## game/index.html line 3133
function buildTrainerParty(t){
  if(t?.guardian)return v83BuildGuardianParty(t);
  const count=trainerPartySize(t),party=[];
  const zoneBase=Math.max(v83AreaLevelBase(),Number(t.level)||1);
  for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
    const lvl=Math.min(82,zoneBase+i);
    const pool=CREATURES.filter(c=>minLevelForSpeciesV81(c.id)<=lvl);
    const seed=Math.abs((t.look||0)*17+lvl*11+i*19)%pool.length;
    const m=mon((pool[seed]||CREATURES[0]).id,lvl);
    m.moves=defaultMoves(m.id,m.level);
    party.push(m)
  }
  return party
}

## game/index.html line 11129
buildTrainerParty=function(t){
  if(t?.guardian||t?.taron||t?.finalBoss||t?.templeGuard||t?.buildingEventV66||t?.buildingEventV67||t?.buildingEventV68||t?.buildingEventV70){
    return _v98BuildTrainerParty(t)
  }
  if(currentScene()?.kind!=='route')return _v98BuildTrainerParty(t);

  const count=v98RoadTrainerPartySize(t);
  const base=v98RoadTrainerLevel(t);
  const party=[];
  const hash=v98Hash(t?.id||'trainer');

  for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
    // Maximum +1 par membre, sans saut brutal.
    const lvl=Math.min(68,base+Math.floor(i/2));
    const pool=CREATURES.filter(c=>minLevelForSpeciesV81(c.id)<=lvl);
    const c=pool[(hash+i*31)%Math.max(1,pool.length)]||CREATURES[0];
    const m=mon(c.id,lvl);
    m.moves=defaultMoves(m.id,m.level);
    party.push(m)
  }
  return party
}

buildTrainerParty=function(t){
  if(t?.taronBossV102Z){
    const count=trainerPartySize(t),route=Number(String(t.zone||state.zone).replace(/\D/g,''))||0;
    const wild=v83AreaLevelBase(),base=Math.min(72,Math.max(wild+2,Math.floor(zAvgLevel())+1));
    const types=['Ombre','Foudre','Feu','Roche'];
    const pool=CREATURES.filter(c=>types.includes(c.type)&&minLevelForSpeciesV81(c.id)<=base+2);
    return Array.from({length:count},(_,i)=>{const c=pool[(zHash(t.id)+i*37)%Math.max(1,pool.length)]||CREATURES[0];const m=mon(c.id,Math.min(72,base+Math.floor(i/2)));m.moves=defaultMoves(m.id,m.level);return m})
  }
  if(currentScene()?.kind==='route'&&!t?.guardian&&!t?.taron&&!t?.finalBoss&&!t?.templeGuard){
    const count=trainerPartySize(t),base=zRouteTrainerLevel(t),party=[],hash=zHash(t?.id||'trainer');
    for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
      const lvl=Math.min(70,base+Math.floor(i/2));const pool=CREATURES.filter(c=>minLevelForSpeciesV81(c.id)<=lvl);
      const c=pool[(hash+i*31)%Math.max(1,pool.length)]||CREATURES[0];const m=mon(c.id,lvl);m.moves=defaultMoves(m.id,m.level);party.push(m)
    }
    return party
  }
  return Z_BUILD_TRAINER_PARTY(t)
}

window.buildTrainerParty=function(t){

  return party
}

## game/index.html line 15174
function enemyDefeated(){
  if(!combatReady())return;
  const defeated=battle.enemy;grantEnemyXP(defeated);
  if(battle.trainer&&battle.trainerParty&&battle.trainerIndex<battle.trainerParty.length-1){
    battle.trainerIndex++;battle.enemy=normalizeCreature(battle.trainerParty[battle.trainerIndex],battle.trainer.level||defeated.level);
    battle.busy=true;battle.phase='trainer-send';battle.menu='actions';setMessage(`Le dresseur envoie ${nameOf(battle.enemy)} !`);safeCall(battleCry,battle.enemy.id);buildButtons();
    schedule(720,()=>{battle.busy=false;battle.phase='trainer-switch';battle.menu='trainer-switch';setMessage(`Le dresseur envoie ${nameOf(battle.enemy)}. Veux-tu garder ${nameOf(currentPlayer())} ou changer de créature ?`);buildButtons()});return
  }
  if(battle.trainer){
    battle.reward=Math.max(50,Math.floor((Number(battle.trainer.level)||defeated.level)*18));
    state.money=(state.money||0)+battle.reward;battle.eventQueue.push(`Récompense : ${voltronLabelV85(battle.reward)}.`)
  }else{
    battle.reward=Math.floor((safeCall(wildRewardV85,defeated)||20)*1.10);
    state.money=(state.money||0)+battle.reward;battle.eventQueue.push(`Combat sauvage : +${voltronLabelV85(battle.reward)}.`)
  }
  if(typeof zCombatLoot==='function'){
    const loot=safeCall(zCombatLoot,battle,defeated);if(Array.isArray(loot))battle.eventQueue.push(...loot)
  }
  battle.result='win';battle.busy=true;setMessage('Victoire !');safeCall(hud);buildButtons();
  schedule(1400,()=>finishV105R('win'))
}

## game/assets/v105r-combat.js line 378
function enemyDefeated(){
  if(!combatReady())return;
  const defeated=battle.enemy;grantEnemyXP(defeated);
  if(battle.trainer&&battle.trainerParty&&battle.trainerIndex<battle.trainerParty.length-1){
    battle.trainerIndex++;battle.enemy=normalizeCreature(battle.trainerParty[battle.trainerIndex],battle.trainer.level||defeated.level);
    battle.busy=true;setMessage(`Le dresseur envoie ${nameOf(battle.enemy)} !`);safeCall(battleCry,battle.enemy.id);
    schedule(720,()=>returnToPlayer());return
  }
  if(battle.trainer){
    battle.reward=Math.max(50,Math.floor((Number(battle.trainer.level)||defeated.level)*18));
    state.money=(state.money||0)+battle.reward;battle.eventQueue.push(`Récompense : ${voltronLabelV85(battle.reward)}.`)
  }else{
    battle.reward=Math.floor((safeCall(wildRewardV85,defeated)||20)*1.10);
    state.money=(state.money||0)+battle.reward;battle.eventQueue.push(`Combat sauvage : +${voltronLabelV85(battle.reward)}.`)
  }
  if(typeof zCombatLoot==='function'){
    const loot=safeCall(zCombatLoot,battle,defeated);if(Array.isArray(loot))battle.eventQueue.push(...loot)
  }
  battle.result='win';battle.busy=true;setMessage('Victoire !');safeCall(hud);buildButtons();
  schedule(850,()=>finishV105R('win'))
}

## game/index.html line 14904
function beginBattle(enemy,trainer=null){
  ensureOrbInventory();preloadOrbImages();
  const player=(state.team||[]).find(alive);
  if(!player){safeCall(toast,'Toute ton équipe est K.O. Va la faire soigner avant de combattre.');return false}
  const priorScene=scene,priorBuilding=building;
  let incomingEnemy=enemy;
  if(!trainer&&state.zone==='route4bis'){
    const lvl=Math.max(1,Number(enemy?.level)||Math.max(3,Number(safeCall(v83AreaLevelBase))||15));
    const originalType=BY?.[Number(enemy?.id)]?.type;
    if(originalType!=='Eau'){
      const waterPool=CREATURES.filter(c=>c.type==='Eau'&&minimumLegalLevel(c.id)<=lvl&&(c.stage||0)<3);
      const water=waterPool[Math.floor(Math.random()*Math.max(1,waterPool.length))]||CREATURES.find(c=>c.type==='Eau'&&(c.stage||0)===0)||CREATURES[0];
      incomingEnemy=mon(water.id,lvl);incomingEnemy.moves=defaultMoves(incomingEnemy.id,lvl)
    }
  }
  const rawEnemy=!trainer?legalizeWildCreatureV106J(incomingEnemy):incomingEnemy;
  const foe=normalizeCreature(rawEnemy,trainer?.level||player.level||5);
  battleToken++;
  battle={
    engine:VERSION,token:battleToken,enemy:foe,trainer:trainer||null,
    trainerParty:Array.isArray(trainer?.party)?trainer.party:null,trainerIndex:Number(trainer?.partyIndex)||0,
    trainerHealUses:0,trainerHealByIndex:{},
    player,participants:[player],busy:true,phase:'intro',menu:'actions',message:'',anim:null,orb:null,
    result:null,reward:0,eventQueue:[],returnScene:priorScene,returnBuilding:priorBuilding,originZone:state.zone
  };
  scene='battle';installInterface();
  const ui=el('battleUI');if(ui){ui.dataset.combatActive='false';ui.style.setProperty('display','none','important')}
  const menu=el('menuov');if(menu)menu.style.display='none';
  const dlg=el('dialog');if(dlg){dlg.classList.remove('show');dlg.style.display='none'}
  const alreadyCaught=!trainer&&!!state.dex?.[foe.id]?.caught;setMessage(trainer?`${trainer.dialog||trainer.name||'Un dresseur'} te défie !`:(state.zone==='town0'?`Un Éclat sauvage apparaît : ${nameOf(foe)} !${alreadyCaught?' — Déjà capturée.':''}`:`Un ${nameOf(foe)} sauvage apparaît !${alreadyCaught?' — Déjà capturée.':''}`));
  playBattleTransitionV106S(trainer,foe,()=>{
    if(!battle||battle.token!==battleToken)return;
    if(ui){ui.dataset.combatActive='true';ui.style.setProperty('display','flex','important')}
    safeCall(startBattleMusic);safeCall(battleCry,foe.id);safeCall(hud);buildButtons();
    schedule(620,()=>{if(!battle)return;battle.busy=false;battle.phase='player';setMessage('À toi de jouer.');buildButtons()})
  });
  return true
}

function beginBattle(enemy,trainer=null){
  ensureOrbInventory();preloadOrbImages();
  const player=(state.team||[]).find(alive);
  if(!player){safeCall(toast,'Toute ton équipe est K.O. Va la faire soigner avant de combattre.');return false}
  const priorScene=scene;
  const priorBuilding=building;
  const foe=normalizeCreature(enemy,trainer?.level||player.level||5);
  battleToken++;
  battle={
    engine:VERSION,token:battleToken,enemy:foe,trainer:trainer||null,
    trainerParty:Array.isArray(trainer?.party)?trainer.party:null,trainerIndex:Number(trainer?.partyIndex)||0,
    player,participants:[player],busy:true,phase:'intro',menu:'actions',message:'',anim:null,orb:null,
    result:null,reward:0,eventQueue:[],returnScene:priorScene,returnBuilding:priorBuilding
  };
  scene='battle';
  installInterface();
  const ui=el('battleUI');if(ui){ui.dataset.combatActive='true';ui.style.setProperty('display','flex','important')}
  const menu=el('menuov');if(menu)menu.style.display='none';
  const dlg=el('dialog');if(dlg){dlg.classList.remove('show');dlg.style.display='none'}
  setMessage(trainer?`${trainer.dialog||trainer.name||'Un dresseur'} te défie !`:`Un ${nameOf(foe)} sauvage apparaît !`);

}

## game/index.html line 14943
function beginTrainer(rawTrainer){
  if(!rawTrainer)return false;
  let t={...rawTrainer};
  const special=t.guardian||t.taron||t.finalBoss||t.templeGuard||t.buildingEventV66||t.buildingEventV67||t.buildingEventV68||t.buildingEventV70;
  if(!special&&safeCall(currentScene)?.kind==='route'&&typeof v98RoadTrainerLevel==='function'){
    t.level=v98RoadTrainerLevel(t);t.zone=state.zone
  }
  if(!t.guardian&&typeof trainerDefeatedV88==='function'&&trainerDefeatedV88(t)){
    safeCall(dialog,`<b>${t.dialog||'Dresseur'}</b><br>Ce combat a déjà été remporté.`);return false
  }
  let party=Array.isArray(t.party)&&t.party.length?t.party:safeCall(buildTrainerParty,t);
  if(!Array.isArray(party)||!party.length)party=[mon(CREATURES[0].id,Math.max(3,Number(t.level)||5))];
  party=party.map(m=>normalizeCreature(m,t.level||5));
  t.party=party;t.partyIndex=0;
  return beginBattle(party[0],t)
}

## game/CREATEUR.html line 14922
function beginTrainer(rawTrainer){
  if(!rawTrainer)return false;
  let t={...rawTrainer};
  const special=t.guardian||t.taron||t.finalBoss||t.templeGuard||t.buildingEventV66||t.buildingEventV67||t.buildingEventV68||t.buildingEventV70;
  if(!special&&safeCall(currentScene)?.kind==='route'&&typeof v98RoadTrainerLevel==='function'){
    t.level=v98RoadTrainerLevel(t);t.zone=state.zone
  }
  if(!t.guardian&&typeof trainerDefeatedV88==='function'&&trainerDefeatedV88(t)){

function beginWild(){

  if(state.zone==='town0'){
    const levels=(state.team||[]).map(m=>Number(m?.level)||0).filter(n=>n>0);
    const avg=levels.length?Math.round(levels.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/levels.length):5;
    const spread=[-2,-1,0,0,1,2],intended=Math.max(3,Math.min(68,avg+spread[Math.floor(Math.random()*spread.length)]));base=intended;
    const profile=wildBiomeProfileV106Q('town0');
    pool=CREATURES.filter(c=>profile.allowed.includes(c.type)&&minimumLegalLevel(c.id)<=intended&&(c.stage||0)<3)
  }else if(state.zone==='route4bis'){
    base=15+Math.min(3,(state.seals||[]).length);

  }else{

    const profile=wildBiomeProfileV106Q(state.zone);
    pool=CREATURES.filter(c=>profile.allowed.includes(c.type)&&minimumLegalLevel(c.id)<=intended&&(c.stage||0)<3)
  }
  // Le niveau est choisi AVANT l'espèce : une évolution ne peut donc jamais être créée sous son seuil.

    return c?.wild!==false&&Number(c.stage||0)===0&&min<=5&&earlyTypes.includes(c.type)&&!(Number(c.id)>=163&&Number(c.id)<=167);
  });

## game/index.html line 15162
function grantEnemyXP(enemy){
  const base=Math.max(10,Math.floor(enemy.level*11+12+(BY[enemy.id]?.stage||0)*8));
  const members=[...new Set((battle.participants||[]).filter(Boolean))],xpLines=[];
  for(const m of members){
    const amount=Math.max(6,Math.floor(base*(m===currentPlayer()?1:.72)));
    const wasKO=m.hp<=0;const events=safeCall(gainXP,m,amount)||[];if(wasKO)m.hp=0;
    const line=`${nameOf(m)} : +${amount} EXP`;
    xpLines.push(line);battle.eventQueue.push(`${nameOf(m)} gagne ${amount} EXP.`,...events)
  }
  battle.xpFlashLines=xpLines;battle.xpFlashUntil=performance.now()+2400;
}

      xpLines.push(`${nameOf(m)} : +${amount} EXP`);battle.eventQueue.push(`${nameOf(m)} gagne ${amount} EXP.`,...events);
    }

    battle.xpFlashLines=xpLines;battle.xpFlashUntil=performance.now()+2400;

    battle.v109wEnemyParticipants=[];
  }

## game/index.html line 6094
function startHealingSequence(){
  if(healingSeq)return;
  healingSeq={start:performance.now(),duration:5000,done:false};
  dialog('<b>Soigneuse Élise</b><br>Très bien, confie-moi ton équipe.<br><br>Le soin complet va prendre quelques instants...',()=>{});
  setTimeout(()=>closeDialog(true),900);

  // soft healing loop melody
  for(let i=0;i<10;i++){
    const f=[440,554,659,554][i%4];
    setTimeout(()=>tone(f,.12,.018,'sine',0,35),i*700)
  }

  setTimeout(()=>{
    if(!healingSeq)return;
    state.team.forEach(m=>{m.hp=maxHP(m);m.status=null;restoreMovePPV106U(m)});
    healingSeq.done=true;
    hud();save(false);

    // completion jingle
    tone(523,.14,.035,'sine',0,40);
    tone(659,.14,.035,'sine',.12,45);
    tone(784,.18,.032,'sine',.24,60);
    tone(1046,.28,.026,'sine',.39,0);

    dialog('<b>Soigneuse Élise</b><br>Et voilà ! Toute ton équipe est de nouveau en pleine forme.',()=>{
      healingSeq=null;
      toast('Équipe entièrement soignée !')
    })
  },5000)
}

  window.joueurBloque=true;state.mobilierStatutV102Y='couche';state.mobilierInstanceIdV102Y=m.instanceId;
  state.mobilierRetourV102Y={x:state.roomX,y:state.roomY,dir:state.dir};state.mobilierSleepEndsV102Y=Date.now()+15000;
  toast('Le dresseur se couche. Repos pendant 15 secondes…');

    healAll();

    const r=state.mobilierRetourV102Y;if(r){state.roomX=r.x;state.roomY=r.y;state.dir=r.dir}
    state.mobilierStatutV102Y='debout';state.mobilierInstanceIdV102Y=null;state.mobilierSleepEndsV102Y=0;window.joueurBloque=false;
    dialog('<b>Repos terminé</b><br>Ton équipe est entièrement soignée et tous les statuts ont disparu.');save(false);
  },15000);
}

## game/index.html line 1873
function v83AwardGuardianSeal(t){
  if(!t?.guardian||!t.sealIndex)return false;
  const seal=t.sealIndex;
  if(!(state.seals||[]).includes(seal)){
    state.seals.push(seal);
    state.seals.sort((a,b)=>a-b);
    if(state.seals.length>=3){state.flags=state.flags||{};state.flags.v119BikeUnlocked=true;}
    logEvent('Gardien vaincu : Sceau '+seal+' obtenu.');
    return true
  }
  return false
}

function openAdventureChallengesV106Y(){
  ensureDiscoveredChallenges();const r=rank();
  let h=`<div class="quest"><b>Rang d'aventure : ${r.name}</b><br>${marks()}/15 maîtrises de ville<br><small>Bonus réseau de bus : -${Math.round(r.discount*100)} % sur les billets.</small></div>`;
  for(let i=0;i<15;i++){
    const z='town'+i;if(!townDiscovered(z))continue;const ch=challengeFor(z),shape=CHALLENGE_SHAPES_V106Y[z]||[],done=challengeDone(z,ch),rw=rewardFor(z);
    h+=`<div class="quest"><b>${cityName(z)} — Défi de maîtrise</b><br>`+shape.map(([k,g])=>{const p=Math.min(g,objectiveProgress(z,k,g,ch));return `${p>=g?'✓':'•'} ${objectiveText(z,k,g)} : <b>${p}/${g}</b>`}).join('<br>')+`<br><small>Récompense : ${rw.money} V + ${rw.qty} ${rw.item}</small>`;
    if(ch.claimed)h+='<br><b>Maîtrise validée ✓</b>';
    else if(done)h+=`<br><button onclick="claimChallengeV106Y('${z}')">Valider la maîtrise</button>`;
    h+='</div>'
  }
  openSimpleMenu('Défis de Valdora',h)
}

  return true
}

## game/index.html line 16576
function v107dBusStop(sc=scNow(),zone=state?.zone){
  if(!sc||sc.kind!=='town')return null;const h=healer(sc);if(!h)return null;
  const w=Number(sc.width)||1800,hh=Number(sc.height)||1100,core=Math.max(70,Number(sc.v105dCore)||100);
  const roadY=Number.isFinite(Number(h.v105dRoadY))?Number(h.v105dRoadY):(Number(h.doorY)||((Number(h.y)||0)+(Number(h.h)||0)))+Math.max(42,core*.32);
  const hx=Number(h.doorX)||((Number(h.x)||0)+(Number(h.w)||0)/2),hy=(Number(h.y)||0)+(Number(h.h)||0)/2;
  const sideSpaceR=w-((Number(h.x)||0)+(Number(h.w)||0)),sideSpaceL=Number(h.x)||0;
  const side=sideSpaceR>=sideSpaceL?1:-1;
  // V107E : l'abri est placé au niveau du trottoir, et non plus au milieu de la chaussée.
  // On le décale aussi légèrement sur le côté du Centre de soins pour laisser sa porte dégagée.
  const lateral=Math.max(78,Math.min(138,(Number(h.w)||200)*.45+44));
  const x=Math.max(78,Math.min(w-78,hx+side*lateral));
  const curbSide=hy<roadY?-1:1;
  const y=Math.max(82,Math.min(hh-58,roadY+curbSide*(core/2+18)));
  return {id:`${zone}_bus_v107e`,type:'bus',x,y,roadY,curbSide,dir:'front'}
}

## game/index.html line 15842
function openBusMenuV106Y(){
  const lines=lineForZone(state.zone),dest=busDestinations(),count=lines.length;
  let h=`<div class="quest"><b>Arrêt de ${cityName(state.zone)}</b><br><b>${count} ligne${count>1?'s':''} desservie${count>1?'s':''}</b><br>${lines.map(l=>l.name).join(' • ')||'Desserte locale'}<br><small>${SCENES?.[state.zone]?.megacity?'Mégalopole : toutes les lignes du réseau passent par cet arrêt.':'Le bus ne dessert que les villes déjà découvertes des lignes de cet arrêt.'}</small></div>`;
  if(!dest.length)h+='<div class="quest">Aucune autre destination connue sur cette ligne pour le moment.</div>';
  for(const d of dest){const fare=busFare(d.distance);h+=`<button class="bagitem" onclick="boardBusV106Y('${d.zone}',${fare})"><b>🚌 ${cityName(d.zone)}</b><br>${d.line}<br><small>${fare} V</small></button>`}
  openSimpleMenu('Réseau de bus',`<div class="baggrid">${h}</div>`)
}

## game/index.html line 8098
function arenaChallenge(ti){
  const sealIndex=Math.max(1,Math.min(7,Number(ti)||1));
  if((state.seals||[]).includes(sealIndex)){
    dialog('<b>Gardien</b><br>Tu as déjà gagné mon Sceau. Reviens me voir si tu veux parler entraînement.');
    return
  }
  const count=v83GuardianPartySize(sealIndex);
  const base=v83GuardianBaseLevel(sealIndex);
  const guardian={
    id:'guardian_seal_'+sealIndex,
    guardian:true,
    sealIndex,
    look:15,
    level:base,
    dialog:'Gardien du Sceau '+sealIndex,
    zone:state.zone
  };
  dialog('<b>Gardien du Sceau '+sealIndex+'</b><br>'+
    'Pour obtenir ce Sceau, tu dois me vaincre. Mon équipe compte <b>'+count+
    ' créatures</b>, autour du niveau <b>'+base+'</b>. Prépare ton équipe !',
    ()=>startTrainer413(guardian))
}

function startRematchV106Y(t){
  const seals=(state.seals||[]).length,avg=Math.max(5,Math.round((state.team||[]).reduce((s,m)=>s+(Number(m.level)||0),0)/Math.max(1,(state.team||[]).length))),base=Math.min(75,Math.max(Number(t.level)||5,avg+1+Math.min(4,seals))),count=Math.min(5,1+Math.ceil(seals/2)),types=rematchPoolTypes(),party=[];
  const minLegal=id=>window.ValdoraCombatV105R?.minimumLegalLevel?.(id)||1;
  for(let i=0;i<count;i++){const lvl=Math.min(78,base+Math.floor(i/2)),pool=CREATURES.filter(c=>types.includes(c.type)&&minLegal(c.id)<=lvl),c=pool[(Math.abs((t.look||0)*17+(t.level||0)*11+i*29+seals*31))%Math.max(1,pool.length)]||CREATURES[0],m=mon(c.id,lvl);m.moves=defaultMoves(m.id,m.level);party.push(m)}
  const clone={...t,id:rematchId(t),dialog:`Revanche — ${t.dialog||'Dresseur'}`,level:base,party,partyIndex:0,rematchV106Y:true,zone:state.zone};$('menuov').style.display='none';window.startBattle(party[0],clone)
}
